It’s been really up and down.\n\nDoctor: In the mornings, do you typically have little to no appetite—like you're just not hungry—rather than simply not having time or motivation to eat?\n\nPatient: Honestly, it’s just really inconsistent. Some days I forget meals completely, and then other nights I’m tearing through takeout and snacks after midnight. That’s the part that scared me enough to come in.\n\nDoctor: Would you say this pattern of skipping meals during the day and eating heavily after midnight has been happening on most days for at least the past three months?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure if it’s been most days for three months exactly.
